        function toggleSpouseDetails() {
            var hasSpouseYes = document.getElementById("rdo_spo3").checked;
            var spouseDetailsDiv = document.getElementById("spo_div1");
            if (hasSpouseYes) {
                spouseDetailsDiv.style.display = "block"; // Show the spouse details
            } else {
                spouseDetailsDiv.style.display = "none"; // Hide the spouse details
                clearTextField();
            }
         }
         function showDependentDetails() {
    var dependentSelect = document.getElementById("ddl_local2");
    var selectedValue = dependentSelect.value;
    var dependentNameDiv = document.getElementById("dependentDetails");
    var dependentNameInput = document.getElementById("txt_dependentname");
    var dependentPositionInput = document.getElementById("txt_dependentposition");

    // Clear other fields every time a selection is made
    $("#txt_depname").val("");
    $("#txt_deppositn").val("");
    $("#txt_relation").val("");

    if (selectedValue !== "0") {
        dependentNameDiv.style.display = "block"; // Show the dependent details
        dependentNameInput.value = "DEPENDANT " + selectedValue; // Set default name based on selection
    } else {
        dependentNameDiv.style.display = "none"; // Hide the dependent details
        dependentNameInput.value = ""; // Clear name input
        dependentPositionInput.value = ""; // Clear position input
    }
}



          function onlyAlphabets(e, t) {

    try {

        if (window.event) {
            var charCode = window.event.keyCode;
        }
        else if (e) {
            var charCode = e.which;
        }
        else { return true; }
        if ((charCode > 64 && charCode < 91) || (charCode > 96 && charCode < 123) || (charCode == 8) || (charCode == 32))
            return true;
        else
            return false;
    }
    catch (err) {
        alert(err.Description);
    }
         }

         function clearTextField() {
             document.getElementById('txt_spousename').value = '';
             document.getElementById('txt_spouseposition').value = '';
              $('input[name="radio_spo2"]').prop('checked', false); // Reset specific group
           $('input[name="radio_spo6"],input[name="radio_spo5"]').prop('checked', false); // Reset other radio buttons if needed


        }






          function dlt_s1(row) {//////////////////used for delete   row
    //alert("delete");
    var i = row.parentNode.parentNode.rowIndex;
    //var rows = document.getElementById("tbl_surety").rows;
    // var a = rows[i].cells[10].innerText

    document.getElementById("tbl_viewba").deleteRow(i);
    var table = document.getElementById('tbl_viewba');
    var n = table.rows.length;

    if (n > 1) {
        //$("#hidedelsuerty").show();
        $("#tbl_viewba").show();
    }
    else {
        // $("#hidedelsuerty").hide();
        $("#tbl_viewba").hide();
    }


         }



         function emp_load() {
    var user = $("[id*=txt_session]").val();

    $.ajax({
        type: "POST",
        contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
        url: "Asset_And_Liability1.aspx/empl_load",
        data: "{ input: '" + user + "' }",
        dataType: "json",
        success: function (Result) {
            Result = Result.d;
            var res = Result.split('~');
            $("#txt_empcode").val(res[0]);
            $("#txt_empnme").val(res[1]);
            
            $("#txt_desi").val(res[2]);
           

        },
        error: function (Result) {
        }
    })

         };

         function add_rect() {
    var dependant = $("#ddl_local2").val();
    var r_name = $("#txt_depname").val();
    var relation_of_emp = $("#txt_deppositn").val();
    var relation = $("#txt_relation").val();

    ////////////////////////------------dependent-------------///////////////////////
    var wrk3 = 33;
    if ($('input[id=rdo_spo7]').is(':checked')) {
        wrk3 = "YES";
    } else if ($('input[id=rdo_spo8]').is(':checked')) {
        wrk3 = "NO";
    }

    var depn;
    if (dependant == 1) {
        depn = "DEPENDANT 1";
    } else if (dependant == 2) {
        depn = "DEPENDANT 2";
    } else if (dependant == 3) {
        depn = "DEPENDANT 3";
    }

    if (r_name == "" || relation_of_emp == "" || dependant == 0 || relation == "" || wrk3 == 33) {
        alert("Please enter all the fields..!");
        return false;
    }

    var jewels1 = $("#ddl_local2 option:selected").text();
    var table1 = document.getElementById('tbl_viewba');

    for (var j = 1; j <= $("#tbl_viewba tr").length - 1; j++) {
        var slno = table1.rows[j].cells[0].innerText;

        if (jewels1 == slno) {
            alert("Already added this dependant !!!");
            clearInputs();
            return false;
        }
    }

    var currentRowCount = $("#tbl_viewba tbody tr").length; // Get the current number of rows in the table

    if (currentRowCount < 4) { // Check if the current row count is less than 3
        // Create a new row markup
        var markup = "<tr><td>" + depn + "</td><td>" + r_name + "</td><td>" + relation_of_emp + "</td><td>" + relation + "</td><td>" + wrk3 + "</td><td><span class='glyphicon glyphicon-trash' onclick='dlt_s1(this);'></span></td></tr>";

        // Append the new row to the table body
        $("#tbl_viewba tbody").append(markup);
        $("#tbl_ba").show(); // Show the table if it's hidden
        $("#tbl_viewba").show(); // Show the table if it's hidden

        // Clear the input fields and radio buttons
        clearInputs();
    } else {
        alert("Add only three dependant details.");
        clearInputs();
    }
}

function clearInputs() {
    // Clear all input fields
    $("#txt_depname").val("");
    $("#txt_deppositn").val("");
    $("#txt_relation").val("");
    $("#ddl_local2").val("0");

    // Reset all radio buttons
    $('input[name="radio_spo3"]').prop('checked', false); // Reset specific group
    $('input[name="radio_spo7"], input[name="radio_spo8"]').prop('checked', false); // Reset other radio buttons if needed
}

function exit() {
            window.location = "../Index/Index.aspx";
        }

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Web;
using System.Web.UI;
using System.Web.UI.WebControls;
using System.Data;
using System.Web.Services;

namespace Ma_AppSuite.ASSETS_AND_LIABILITY
{
    public partial class Asset_And_Liability1 : System.Web.UI.Page

    {
        static string usr = "" ;
        static string ip="";
        static string hname = "";
        protected void Page_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Session["username"] as string))
            {
                Response.Redirect("~/Login.aspx");
            }
            else
            {
                usr = Session["username"].ToString();
                ip = Session["ipaddress"].ToString();
                hname = Session["hostname"].ToString();
            }
            this.txt_session.Text = usr.ToString();

        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string empl_load(string input)
        {
            string result = "";
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds1 = new DataTable();
            ds1 = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "1", input);
            if (ds1.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                result = ds1.Rows[0][0].ToString() + "~" + ds1.Rows[0][1].ToString() + "~" + ds1.Rows[0][2].ToString() ;
            }
            return result;
        }
        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string createnew(string input)
        {


            string s = "";

            string data = usr + "~" + ip + "~" + hname + "~" + input;
            // string fdat =  ;
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "2", data);
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();

            }





            return s;
        }



    }
    }

         });
          function validate(input){
     if(/^\s/.test(input.value))
      input.value = '';
            }
         function onlyAlphabets(e, t) {

             try {

                 if (window.event) {
                     var charCode = window.event.keyCode;
                 }
                 else if (e) {
                     var charCode = e.which;
                 }
                 else { return true; }
                 if ((charCode > 64 && charCode < 91) || (charCode > 96 && charCode < 123) || (charCode == 8) || (charCode == 32))
                     return true;
                 else
                     return false;
             }
             catch (err) {
                 alert(err.Description);
             }
         }


         function populateYearPicker() {
    const yearSelect = document.getElementById('txt_YR_PUR');
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    const startYear = currentYear - 100; // 100 years back
    const endYear = currentYear; // Current year only

    for (let year = startYear; year <= endYear; year++) {
        const option = document.createElement('option');
        option.value = year;
        option.textContent = year;
        yearSelect.appendChild(option);
    }
}


         // Function to display the selected year
         function displaySelectedYear() {
             const yearSelect = document.getElementById('txt_YR_PUR');
             const selectedYear = yearSelect.value;
             document.getElementById('selected-year').textContent = `You selected: ${selectedYear}`;
         }
         function isNumberKey(evt, element) {

             var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : event.keyCode
             if (charCode > 31 && (charCode < 48 || charCode > 57) && !(charCode == 8))
                 return false;
         }


        function IsAlphaNumeric(event) {
    const char = String.fromCharCode(event.which);
    return /^[a-zA-Z0-9]*$/.test(char);
}



         function clearTableRows() {
             var tableBody = document.getElementById("tbl_viewba");
             if (tableBody) {
                 while (tableBody.rows.length > 0) {
                     tableBody.deleteRow(1);  // Always delete the first row until the table is empty
                 }
             }
         }
         function clearTableRows1() {
             var tableBody = document.getElementById("tbl_viewba1");
             if (tableBody) {
                 while (tableBody.rows.length > 0) {
                     tableBody.deleteRow(1);  // Always delete the first row until the table is empty
                 }
             }
         }

         function dlt_s1(row) {//////////////////used for delete   row
             //alert("delete");
             var i = row.parentNode.parentNode.rowIndex;
             //var rows = document.getElementById("tbl_surety").rows;
             // var a = rows[i].cells[10].innerText

             document.getElementById("tbl_viewba").deleteRow(i);
             var table = document.getElementById('tbl_viewba');
             var n = table.rows.length;

             if (n > 1) {
                 //$("#hidedelsuerty").show();
                 $("#tbl_viewba").show();
             }
             else {
                 // $("#hidedelsuerty").hide();
                 $("#tbl_viewba").hide();
             }


         }

         function toggleSpouseDetails() {
             var hasSpouseYes = document.getElementById("rdo_spo3").checked;
             var spouseDetailsDiv = document.getElementById("spo_div1");
             if (hasSpouseYes) {
                 spouseDetailsDiv.style.display = "block"; // Show the spouse details
             } else {
                 spouseDetailsDiv.style.display = "none"; // Hide the spouse details
                 clearTableRows1();
             }
         }

         function toggleSpouseDetails1() {
             var hasSpouseYes = document.getElementById("rdo_spo6").checked;

             } else {
                 spouseDetailsDiv.style.display = "none"; // Hide the spouse details

         function add_rect1() {


             var investment = $("#ddl_local2").val();
             var amount = $("#txt_amount").val();
             var remark = $("#txt_rmrk1").val();

             var investment1 = $("#ddl_local2 option:selected").text();


             if (investment == 0 || amount == "" || remark == "") {
                 alert("Please enter all the fields..!");
                 return false;
             }

             var table = document.getElementById('tbl_viewba');
             for (j = 1; j <= $("#tbl_viewba tr").length - 1; j++) {

                 var slno = table.rows[j].cells[0].innerText;
                 if (investment1 == slno) {
                     alert("Already added this investment !!!");
                     return false;
                 }
             }


             var markup = "<tr><td>" + investment1 + "</td><td>" + amount + "</td><td>" + remark + "</td><td><span class='glyphicon glyphicon-trash' onclick='dlt_s1(this);'></span></td></tr>";


             $("#tbl_viewba tbody").append(markup);
             $("#tbl_ba").show();
             $("#tbl_viewba").show();
             $("#txt_amount").val("");
             $("#txt_rmrk1").val("");
             $("#ddl_local2").val(0);

         }

         function empassgn_add() {

             $("#ddl_emp").empty();

             var empid = $("[id*=txt_session]").val();

             $.ajax({
                 type: "POST",
                 contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                 url: "Movable_forms.aspx/assign_dtl",
                 data: "{ input: '" + empid + "' }",
                 dataType: "json",
                 success: function (Result) {
                     Result = Result.d;
                     $.each(Result, function (key, value) {
                         $("#ddl_emp").append($("<option></option>").val
                             (value.e_code).html(value.e_name));
                     });
                 },
                 error: function (Result) {
                 }
             });
         }


         function getdata() {
             var dep = $('#ddl_emp option:selected').text();
             var seq = dep.split('-');
             var seq1 = seq[2];
             var empid = $("[id*=txt_session]").val();

             var input = seq1 + "~" + empid;
             //alert(input);
             $.ajax({
                 type: "POST",
                 contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                 url: "Movable_forms.aspx/GetData",
                 data: "{ input: '" + input + "'}",
                 dataType: "json",
                 success: function (Result) {
                     var dat = Result.d.split("~");
                     $('#ddl_dep_name').val(dat[0]);
                     $('#ddl_dep_pos').val(dat[1]);




                 }
             });
         }

                                 }

         function exit() {
            window.location = "../Index/Index.aspx";

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Web;
using System.Web.UI;
using System.Web.UI.WebControls;
using System.Data;
using System.Web.Services;


namespace Ma_AppSuite.ASSETS_AND_LIABILITY
{
    public partial class Movable_forms : System.Web.UI.Page
    {
        static string usr = "";
        protected void Page_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Session["username"] as string))
            {
                Response.Redirect("~/Login.aspx");
            }
            else
            {
                usr = Session["username"].ToString();
               
            }
            this.txt_session.Text = usr.ToString();
            
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
          
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();

            dt = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "11", usr);

            this.hdvalue.Text = dt.Rows[0][0].ToString();



        }

        public class e_assgn
        {
            public string e_code { get; set; }
            public string e_name { get; set; }
        }
        public List<e_assgn> emp_assign { get; set; }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static List<e_assgn> assign_dtl(string input)
        {
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            List<e_assgn> emp_assign = new List<e_assgn>();
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();

            dt = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "3", input);
            try
            {
                if (dt.Rows.Count > 0)
                {
                    foreach (DataRow dr in dt.Rows)
                    {
                        emp_assign.Add(new e_assgn()
                        {
                            e_code = dr["dependant"].ToString(),
                            e_name = dr["dependant_name"].ToString()
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
            }
            return emp_assign;
        }
        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string GetData(string input)
        {
            string data = "";
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            dt = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "4", input);
            if (dt.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                data = dt.Rows[0][0].ToString() + "~" + dt.Rows[0][1].ToString();

            }
            return data;
        }
        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string createnew1(string input)
        {

            
            string s = "";

            string data = input;
            // string fdat =  ;
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "5", data);
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();

            }





            return s;
        }

    }
} and 3rd page is <%@ Page Title="" Language="C#" MasterPageFile="~/Main.Master" AutoEventWireup="true" CodeBehind="Immovable_forms.aspx.cs" Inherits="Ma_AppSuite.ASSETS_AND_LIABILITY.Immovable_forms" %>

<asp:Content ID="Content1" ContentPlaceHolderID="head" runat="server">
    <script type="text/javascript">
        window.onload = function () {
            check2();
            dept_dtl();
            $("#ddl_emp").change(function () {
                var selectedValue = $(this).val(); // Get the selected value
                onDependentChange(selectedValue); // Call the function to handle the change
            });

            $("#txt_STATE").change(function () {
                var selectedValue = $(this).val(); // Get the selected value
                onStateChange(selectedValue); // Call the function to handle the change
            });
        };
        function check2() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                url: "Immovable_forms.aspx/checkpending", // Ensure this matches your server-side method name
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: "your_input_value" }), // Pass any required input
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    // Check the response
                    if (response.d === "111") { // Assuming response.d contains the returned value
                        
                    } else {
                        alert("You have pending in Movable page!");
                        window.location = "../ASSETS_AND_LIABILITY/movable_forms.aspx";
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error("Error details:", xhr.responseText); // Log error details
                    alert("Error: " + error);
                }
            });
        }
        

        function dept_dtl() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                url: "Immovable_forms.aspx/LoadDependents", // Ensure this matches your server-side method name
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: "your_input_value" }), // Pass any required input
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    var result = response.d;
                    var ddlEmp = $("#ddl_emp");
                    ddlEmp.empty();

                    if (Array.isArray(result) && result.length > 1) {
                        result.forEach(function (item) {
                            ddlEmp.append($("<option></option>").val(item.DependentId).text(item.DependentName));
                        });
                    } else {
                        ///alert('You have successfully completed this page.');
                        window.location = "../ASSETS_AND_LIABILITY/DEPT_AND_LIABILITY.aspx";
                    }
                },
                error: function (response) {
                    alert('Error: ' + response.responseText);
                }
            });
        }

        function onDependentChange(selectedValue) {
            document.getElementById('propertyTable').style.display = 'none';
            var mainDiv = $("#maindiv"); // Use the correct ID for your main div
            document.getElementById('insertbutton').style.display = 'none';
            //clearTableRows();
            if (selectedValue === "-1") {
                // Hide the main div if the selected value is -1
                mainDiv.hide();
                clearFormFields();
                clearTableRows();
            } else {
                // Show the main div for any other selected value
                mainDiv.show();
                document.getElementById('insertbutton').style.display = 'block';
                $.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    url: "Immovable_forms.aspx/Loaddata", // Ensure this matches your server-side method name
                    data: JSON.stringify({ input: selectedValue }), // Pass any required input
                    dataType: "json",
                    success: function (Result) {
                        var res = Result.d.split("~");
                        $('#dependantName').val(res[0]);
                        $('#position').val(res[1]);
                        clearTableRows();
                    },
                    error: function (response) {
                        alert('Error: ' + response.responseText);
                    }
                });
            }

        }

        function onStateChange(selectedValue) {
            if (selectedValue === "-1") {
                // Handle the case where the selected value is -1 if needed
            } else {
                // Show the main div for any other selected value
                $.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    url: "Immovable_forms.aspx/LoadDistrict", // Ensure this matches your server-side method name
                    data: JSON.stringify({ input: selectedValue }), // Pass any required input
                    dataType: "json",
                    success: function (Result) {
                        var result = Result.d; // Corrected from response.d to Result.d
                        var ddlDis = $("#txt_DIS");
                        ddlDis.empty(); // Corrected from ddlSta to ddlDis

                        if (Array.isArray(result) && result.length > 0) {
                            result.forEach(function (item) {
                                ddlDis.append($("<option></option>").val(item.DistrictId).text(item.DistrictName));
                            });
                        } else {
                            alert('Error: No data returned');
                        }
                    },
                    error: function (response) {
                        alert('Error: ' + response.responseText);
                    }
                });
            }
        }


        function togglePropertyDiv() {
            var yesRadio = document.getElementById("1");
            var propertyDiv = document.getElementById("propertydiv");
            var radioDiv = document.getElementById("radio");
            document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('acq').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('house').style.display = 'none';
            //document.getElementById('flat').style.display = 'none';
            //document.getElementById('shop').style.display = 'none';
            //document.getElementById('industries').style.display = 'none';
            //document.getElementById('other').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('detls').style.display = 'none';

            document.getElementById('propertyTable').style.display = 'none';

            document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'none';


            // Reset all fields to default values
            function resetFields() {
                // Reset text inputs
                var textInputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[type="text"]');
                textInputs.forEach(function (input) {
                    if (input.id !== 'dependantName' && input.id !== 'position') {
                        input.value = ''; // Clear the value
                    }
                });

                // Reset dropdowns to the first option
                var dropdowns = document.querySelectorAll('select');
                dropdowns.forEach(function (select) {
                    if (select.id !== 'ddl_emp') {
                        select.selectedIndex = 0; // Set to the first option
                    }
                });
            }

            if (yesRadio.checked) {
                propertyDiv.style.display = "block"; // Show property div
                clearTableRows();
                resetFields();
            } else {
                propertyDiv.style.display = "none"; // Hide property div
                radioDiv.style.display = "block"; // Show radio div
                document.getElementById('land').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('acq').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('house').style.display = 'none';
                //document.getElementById('flat').style.display = 'none';
                //document.getElementById('shop').style.display = 'none';
                //document.getElementById('industries').style.display = 'none';
                //document.getElementById('other').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('detls').style.display = 'none';

                document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'none';

                resetFields();

            }
            staterow();
        }


        function clearTableRows() {
            var tableBody = document.getElementById("propertyTable");
            if (tableBody) {
                while (tableBody.rows.length > 0) {
                    tableBody.deleteRow(1);  // Always delete the first row until the table is empty
                }
            }
        }

        function staterow() {
            // AJAX call to fetch state and district values
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                url: "Immovable_forms.aspx/LoadState", // Ensure this matches your server-side method name
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: "input_value" }), // Pass any required input
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    var result = response.d;
                    var ddlSta = $("#txt_STATE");
                    ddlSta.empty();

                    if (Array.isArray(result) && result.length > 0) {
                        result.forEach(function (item) {
                            ddlSta.append($("<option></option>").val(item.StateId).text(item.StateName));
                        });
                    } else {
                        alert('Error: No data returned');
                    }
                },
                error: function (response) {
                    alert('Error: ' + response.responseText);
                }
            });
        }


        function showProptDetails() {
            // Hide all sections initially
            document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('acq').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('house').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('detls').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'none';

            staterow();

            function resetFields() {
                // Reset text inputs
                var textInputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[type="text"]');
                textInputs.forEach(function (input) {
                    if (input.id !== 'dependantName' && input.id !== 'position') {
                        input.value = ''; // Clear the value
                    }
                });

                // Reset dropdowns to the first option
                // Reset specific dropdowns to the first option
                document.getElementById('d_typ').selectedIndex = 0; // Type
                document.getElementById('d_land').selectedIndex = 0; // Type of Land
                document.getElementById('ddl_acquisition').selectedIndex = 0; // How Acquired
            }

            // Add more sections to hide as needed

            // Get the selected value
            var selectedValue = document.getElementById('ddl_PROP').value;

            // Show the relevant section based on the selected value
            switch (selectedValue) {
                case '1': // Insurance

                    document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'block';

                    document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'block';

                    break;
                case '2': // Fixed/Recurring Deposit
                    document.getElementById('house').style.display = 'block';
                    break;
                case '3': // Insurance
                    //document.getElementById('flat').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('detls').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('rmkrent').style.display = 'none'; // Hide rmkrent
                    document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('acq').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'block';
                    break;
                case '4': // Insurance
                    //document.getElementById('shop').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('detls').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('rmkrent').style.display = 'none'; // Hide rmkrent
                    document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('acq').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'block';
                    break;
                case '5': // Insurance
                    //document.getElementById('industries').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('detls').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('rmkrent').style.display = 'none'; // Hide rmkrent
                    document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('acq').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'block';
                    break;
                case '6': // Insurance
                    //document.getElementById('other').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('detls').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('rmkrent').style.display = 'none'; // Hide rmkrent
                    document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('acq').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'block';

        function showtypdtl() {
            // Hide all sections initially
            document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('areaofland').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('acq').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('rmkrent').style.display = 'none';

            function resetFields() {
                // Reset text inputs
                var textInputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[type="text"]');
                textInputs.forEach(function (input) {
                    if (input.id !== 'dependantName' && input.id !== 'position') {
                        input.value = ''; // Clear the value
                    }
                });
                document.getElementById('d_land').selectedIndex = 0; // Type of Land
                document.getElementById('ddl_acquisition').selectedIndex = 0; // How Acquired
            }
            // Add more sections to hide as needed

            // Get the selected value
            var selectedtyp = document.getElementById('d_typ').value;

            // Show the relevant section based on the selected value
            switch (selectedtyp) {
                case '1': // Insurance
                    document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'block';

                    document.getElementById('rmkrent').style.display = 'block';

                    break;
                case '2': // Fixed/Recurring Deposit
                    document.getElementById('disdivtha').style.display = 'block';

                    document.getElementById('rmkrent').style.display = 'block';

                // Add more cases for other investment types
                default:
                    break;

        }

        function exit() {
            window.location = "../Index/Index.aspx";
        }


        function insert() {
            var tableBody = document.getElementById("propertyTable");
            var radioYes = document.getElementById("1");
            var radioNo = document.getElementById("2");


            // Check if the "Yes" radio button is selected
            if (radioYes.checked) {
                // Check if there are any rows in the table body
                if (tableBody.rows.length === 1) {
                    alert("No rows have been inserted. Please add at least one row before proceeding.");
                    return; // Exit the function to prevent the insert operation
                }
                else {
                    insrttbldb();
                }
            }
            else if (radioNo.checked)
                // If "No" is selected 
                insertnoradio();
            else {
                alert("Please select at least one radio button before proceeding.");
            }
        }

        function insrttbldb() {

            var ddlEmp = document.getElementById('ddl_emp');

            // Get the selected value
            var selecteddepValue = ddlEmp.value;

            // Get the selected text
            var selecteddepText = ddlEmp.options[ddlEmp.selectedIndex].text;
            var values = selecteddepText.split('-'); // Use split method directly on the string
            var dependent = values[0].trim(); // Use trim() to remove any leading/trailing whitespace

            var dataToInsert = ""; // Initialize an empty string to hold the concatenated data

            // Loop through each row starting from index 1 to skip the header
            for (var j = 1; j < $("#propertyTable tr").length; j++) {
                // Get the current row
                var row = $("#propertyTable tr").eq(j);

                // Get all cells in the current row
                var cells = row.find("td"); // Assuming you are using <td> for table data cells

                // Check if the row has the expected number of cells
                if (cells.length < 18) {
                    console.warn("Row " + j + " does not have enough cells.");
                    continue; // Skip this row if it doesn't have enough cells
                }

                // Extract data from each cell
                var property_type = $(cells[0]).text();
                var details = $(cells[1]).text();
                var state_id = $(cells[2]).text();
                var district_id = $(cells[3]).text();
                var division = $(cells[4]).text();
                var thaluk = $(cells[5]).text();
                var village = $(cells[6]).text();
                var distinctive_number = $(cells[7]).text();
                var total_area = $(cells[8]).text();
                var type_of_land = $(cells[9]).text();
                var how_acquired = $(cells[10]).text();
                var name_of_person_from_whom_acq = $(cells[11]).text();
                var address = $(cells[12]).text();
                var connection_to_emp = $(cells[13]).text();
                var cost_of_acq = $(cells[14]).text();
                var present_value = $(cells[15]).text();
                var annual_income = $(cells[16]).text();
                var remark = $(cells[17]).text();

                // Concatenate the data into the desired format
                dataToInsert = dataToInsert + property_type + "*" + details + "*" + state_id + "*" + district_id + "*" + division + "*" +
                    thaluk + "*" + village + "*" + distinctive_number + "*" + total_area + "*" +
                    type_of_land + "*" + how_acquired + "*" + name_of_person_from_whom_acq + "*" +
                    address + "*" + connection_to_emp + "*" + cost_of_acq + "*" +
                    present_value + "*" + annual_income + "*" + remark + "!"; // Add a newline for each row
            }

            data = dependent + "#" + selecteddepValue + "#" + dataToInsert;
            //alert(data);
            insrtajx(data);
        }

        function insrtajx(data) {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "Immovable_forms.aspx/inserttodb",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: data }), // Use JSON.stringify
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    // Check the response
                    if (response.d === "111") { // Assuming response.d contains the returned value
                        alert("Data inserted successfully!");
                        location.reload();
                    } else {
                        alert("Data is already inserted!");
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error("Error details:", xhr.responseText); // Log error details
                    alert("Error: " + error);
                }
            });
        }

        function insertnoradio() {
            var ddlEmp = document.getElementById('ddl_emp');

            // Get the selected value
            var selecteddepValue = ddlEmp.value;

            // Get the selected text
            var selecteddepText = ddlEmp.options[ddlEmp.selectedIndex].text;
            var values = selecteddepText.split('-'); // Use split method directly on the string
            var dependent = values[0].trim(); // Use trim() to remove any leading/trailing whitespace
            data = dependent + "~" + selecteddepValue;
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "Immovable_forms.aspx/insertnodb",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: data }), // Use JSON.stringify
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    // Check the response
                    if (response.d === "111") { // Assuming response.d contains the returned value
                        alert("Data inserted successfully!");
                        location.reload();
                    } else {
                        alert("Data is already inserted!");
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error("Error details:", xhr.responseText); // Log error details
                    alert("Error: " + error);
                }
            });
        }

        function add_prop() {
            // Check if the dependent dropdown is selected
            var dependentDropdown = document.getElementById('ddl_emp');
            if (dependentDropdown.value === '0') {
                alert("Please select a dependent.");
                return; // Exit the function if the condition is not met
            }

            var disdropdown = document.getElementById('txt_DIS').value;
            var statedropdown = document.getElementById('txt_STATE').value;
            if (statedropdown === '-1') {
                alert("Please select a state.");
                return; // Exit the function if the condition is not met
            }
            else if (disdropdown === '-1') {
                alert("Please select a district.");
                return; // Exit the function if the condition is not met
            }

            // Check if the radio button for property ownership is selected
            var yesRadio = document.getElementById("1");
            var noRadio = document.getElementById("2"); // Assuming you have a "No" radio button with id "2"
            if (!yesRadio.checked && !noRadio.checked) {
                alert("Please select whether you own property or not.");
                return; // Exit the function if the condition is not met
            }

            // Check if the radio button for property ownership is "Yes"
            var propertyDropdown = document.getElementById('ddl_PROP');
            if (yesRadio.checked && propertyDropdown.value === '0') {
                alert("Please select a property.");
                return; // Exit the function if the condition is not met
            }

            // Check if the property dropdown is selected and other fields are empty
            if (propertyDropdown.value !== '0') {
                var propertyType = propertyDropdown.value;

                // Check for required fields based on property type
                if (propertyType === '2') { // House
                    var typeDropdown = document.getElementById('d_typ');
                    if (typeDropdown.value === '0') {
                        alert("Please select a type for the house.");
                        return;
                    }
                    if (typeDropdown.value === '1' || typeDropdown.value === '3') { // Rent or another type that requires similar checks // Rent
                        var rentRequiredFields = [
                            'txt_DIS', 'txt_division', 'txt_thaluk', 'txt_village', 'txt_DISNO', 'txt_remarkrendoth'
                        ];
                        for (var i = 0; i < rentRequiredFields.length; i++) {
                            var field = document.getElementById(rentRequiredFields[i]);
                            if (field && field.value.trim() === '') {
                                alert("Please fill in all required fields for Rent.");
                                return;
                            }
                        }

                        //change for hosue rent and other
                        var propvalue = propertyDropdown.options[propertyDropdown.selectedIndex].text;
                        var housetypm = document.getElementById('d_typ');
                        var hosuetyp = housetypm.options[housetypm.selectedIndex].text;



                        // Add the property to the table
                        var table = document.getElementById("propertyTable");
                        var newRow = table.insertRow(-1);
                        newRow.insertCell(0).innerText = propvalue; // Property Type
                        newRow.insertCell(1).innerText = hosuetyp; // typ Details
                        newRow.insertCell(2).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_STATE').value; // District
                        newRow.insertCell(3).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_DIS').value; // District
                        newRow.insertCell(4).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_division').value; // Division
                        newRow.insertCell(5).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_thaluk').value; // Thaluk
                        newRow.insertCell(6).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_village').value; // Village
                        newRow.insertCell(7).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_DISNO').value; // Distinctive Number
                        newRow.insertCell(8).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(9).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(10).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(11).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(12).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(13).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(14).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(15).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(16).innerText = "0";
                        newRow.insertCell(17).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_remarkrendoth').value; // Remark
                        // Show the table if it's hidden

                        var deleteCell = newRow.insertCell(18); // Add a new cell for the delete icon
                        var deleteIcon = document.createElement("span");
                        deleteIcon.className = "glyphicon glyphicon-trash"; // Bootstrap glyphicon for trash
                        deleteIcon.style.cursor = "pointer"; // Change cursor to pointer
                        deleteIcon.onclick = function () {
                            deleteRow(newRow);
                        };
                        deleteCell.appendChild(deleteIcon);

                        table.style.display = "table";

                        // Optionally, clear the form fields after adding
                        clearFormFieldsrent();
                        staterow();
                        /////-------------------------///
                    } else if (typeDropdown.value === '2') { // Owned
                        var ownedRequiredFields = [
                            'txt_acquired_from', 'txt_acquired_from_address', 'txt_connection',
                            'txt_cost_of_acquisition', 'txt_present_value', 'txt_annual_income',
                            'txt_remarkland'
                        ];
                        for (var i = 0; i < ownedRequiredFields.length; i++) {
                            var field = document.getElementById(ownedRequiredFields[i]);
                            if (field && field.value.trim() === '') {
                                alert("Please fill in all required fields for Owned property.");
                                return;
                            }
                        }

                        // Check if the land type dropdown is selected for owned house
                        var landTypeDropdown = document.getElementById('d_land');
                        if (landTypeDropdown.value === '0') {
                            alert("Please select a type of land for the owned house.");
                            return;
                        }

                        // Check if the acquisition method dropdown is selected for owned house
                        var acquisitionDropdown = document.getElementById('ddl_acquisition');
                        if (acquisitionDropdown.value === '0') {
                            alert("Please select how the property was acquired for the owned house.");
                            return;
                        }
                        ///chege owned//
                        var propvalue = propertyDropdown.options[propertyDropdown.selectedIndex].text;
                        var housetypm = document.getElementById('d_typ');
                        var hosuetyp = housetypm.options[housetypm.selectedIndex].text;
                        var landTypeDropdown1 = document.getElementById('d_land');
                        var acquisitionDropdown1 = document.getElementById('ddl_acquisition');
                        var typoflandText = landTypeDropdown1.options[landTypeDropdown1.selectedIndex].text;
                        var howacqText = acquisitionDropdown1.options[acquisitionDropdown1.selectedIndex].text;

                        // Add the property to the table
                        var table = document.getElementById("propertyTable");
                        var newRow = table.insertRow(-1);
                        newRow.insertCell(0).innerText = propvalue; // Property Type
                        newRow.insertCell(1).innerText = hosuetyp; // Details
                        newRow.insertCell(2).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_STATE').value; // District
                        newRow.insertCell(3).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_DIS').value; // District
                        newRow.insertCell(4).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_division').value; // Division
                        newRow.insertCell(5).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_thaluk').value; // Thaluk
                        newRow.insertCell(6).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_village').value; // Village
                        newRow.insertCell(7).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_DISNO').value; // Distinctive Number
                        newRow.insertCell(8).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_trea').value; // Total Area of Land
                        newRow.insertCell(9).innerText = typoflandText; // Type of Land
                        newRow.insertCell(10).innerText = howacqText; // How Acquired
                        newRow.insertCell(11).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_acquired_from').value; // Name of Person/Persons from Whom Acquired
                        newRow.insertCell(12).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_acquired_from_address').value; // Address
                        newRow.insertCell(13).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_connection').value; // Connection to Employee
                        newRow.insertCell(14).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_cost_of_acquisition').value; // Cost of Acquisition
                        newRow.insertCell(15).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_present_value').value; // Present Value
                        newRow.insertCell(16).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_annual_income').value; // Annual Income
                        newRow.insertCell(17).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_remarkland').value; // Remark
                        // Show the table if it's hidden

                        var deleteCell = newRow.insertCell(18); // Add a new cell for the delete icon
                        var deleteIcon = document.createElement("span");
                        deleteIcon.className = "glyphicon glyphicon-trash"; // Bootstrap glyphicon for trash
                        deleteIcon.style.cursor = "pointer"; // Change cursor to pointer
                        deleteIcon.onclick = function () {
                            deleteRow(newRow);
                        };
                        deleteCell.appendChild(deleteIcon);

                        table.style.display = "table";

                        // Optionally, clear the form fields after adding
                        clearFormFieldsadd();
                        staterow();

                        //done///

                    }
                } else if (['1', '3', '4', '5', '6'].includes(propertyType)) { // Land, Flat, Shop, Industries, Other
                    var otherRequiredFields = [
                        'txt_DIS', 'txt_division', 'txt_thaluk', 'txt_village', 'txt_DISNO', 'txt_trea',
                        'd_land', 'ddl_acquisition', 'txt_acquired_from', 'txt_acquired_from_address', 'txt_connection',
                        'txt_cost_of_acquisition', 'txt_present_value', 'txt_annual_income', 'txt_remarkland'
                    ];
                    for (var i = 0; i < otherRequiredFields.length; i++) {
                        var field = document.getElementById(otherRequiredFields[i]);
                        if (field && field.value.trim() === '') {
                            alert("Please fill in all required fields for the selected property type.");
                            return;
                        }
                    }

                    // Check if the land type dropdown is selected
                    var landTypeDropdown = document.getElementById('d_land');
                    if (landTypeDropdown.value === '0') {
                        alert("Please select a type of land.");
                        return;
                    }

                    // Check if the acquisition method dropdown is selected
                    var acquisitionDropdown = document.getElementById('ddl_acquisition');
                    if (acquisitionDropdown.value === '0') {
                        alert("Please select how the property was acquired.");
                        return;
                    }


                    var landTypeDropdown1 = document.getElementById('d_land');
                    var acquisitionDropdown1 = document.getElementById('ddl_acquisition');


                    var propertyTypeText = propertyDropdown.options[propertyDropdown.selectedIndex].text;
                    var typoflandText = landTypeDropdown1.options[landTypeDropdown1.selectedIndex].text;
                    var howacqText = acquisitionDropdown1.options[acquisitionDropdown1.selectedIndex].text;
                    var detailsValue = document.getElementById('txt_dtl').value; // Get the value from the Details input field
                    // If the property type is "Land" or "House" and details are not provided, set it to the selected property type text
                    if ((propertyType === '1' || propertyType === '2') && detailsValue === '') {
                        detailsValue = propertyTypeText; // Set details to the selected property type text
                    }
                    // Add the property to the table
                    var table = document.getElementById("propertyTable");
                    var newRow = table.insertRow(-1);
                    newRow.insertCell(0).innerText = propertyTypeText; // Property Type
                    newRow.insertCell(1).innerText = detailsValue; // Details
                    newRow.insertCell(2).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_STATE').value; // District
                    newRow.insertCell(3).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_DIS').value; // District
                    newRow.insertCell(4).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_division').value; // Division
                    newRow.insertCell(5).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_thaluk').value; // Thaluk
                    newRow.insertCell(6).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_village').value; // Village
                    newRow.insertCell(7).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_DISNO').value; // Distinctive Number
                    newRow.insertCell(8).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_trea').value; // Total Area of Land
                    newRow.insertCell(9).innerText = typoflandText; // Type of Land
                    newRow.insertCell(10).innerText = howacqText; // How Acquired
                    newRow.insertCell(11).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_acquired_from').value; // Name of Person/Persons from Whom Acquired
                    newRow.insertCell(12).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_acquired_from_address').value; // Address
                    newRow.insertCell(13).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_connection').value; // Connection to Employee
                    newRow.insertCell(14).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_cost_of_acquisition').value; // Cost of Acquisition
                    newRow.insertCell(15).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_present_value').value; // Present Value
                    newRow.insertCell(16).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_annual_income').value; // Annual Income
                    newRow.insertCell(17).innerText = document.getElementById('txt_remarkland').value; // Remark
                    // Show the table if it's hidden

                    var deleteCell = newRow.insertCell(18); // Add a new cell for the delete icon
                    var deleteIcon = document.createElement("span");
                    deleteIcon.className = "glyphicon glyphicon-trash"; // Bootstrap glyphicon for trash
                    deleteIcon.style.cursor = "pointer"; // Change cursor to pointer
                    deleteIcon.onclick = function () {
                        deleteRow(newRow);
                    };
                    deleteCell.appendChild(deleteIcon);

                    table.style.display = "table";

                    // Optionally, clear the form fields after adding
                    clearFormFieldsadd();
                    staterow();
                }
            }

            // If all checks pass, proceed with the insert operation
            alert("Row Added.");
        }
        function deleteRow(row) {
            var table = document.getElementById("propertyTable");
            table.deleteRow(row.rowIndex); // Delete the row from the table
            alert("Row Deleted."); // Optional: Alert the user

            // Check if the table should be hidden
            if (table.rows.length <= 1) {
                table.style.display = "none"; // Hide the table if no rows are left
            }
        }

        function validateNumber(input) {
            const pattern = /^[0-9\-\/_]+$/; // Only numbers, hyphen, slash, and underscore
            if (!pattern.test(input.value)) {
                alert("Please enter a valid Distinctive Number.");
                input.focus();
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
        function isNumberKey(evt) {
            var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : evt.keyCode;
            // Allow only numbers (0-9)
            if (charCode > 31 && (charCode < 48 || charCode > 57)) {
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
        function isValidCharacter(evt) {
            var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : evt.keyCode;
            // Allow numbers (0-9), hyphen (-), slash (/), and underscore (_)
            if (charCode > 31 && (charCode < 48 || charCode > 57) && charCode !== 45 && charCode !== 47 && charCode !== 95) {
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }

        function isValidChar(evt) {
            var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : evt.keyCode;
            // Allow alphabetic characters (A-Z, a-z), hyphen (-), space, and underscore (_)
            if (charCode > 31 &&
                !((charCode >= 65 && charCode <= 90) || // A-Z
                    (charCode >= 97 && charCode <= 122) || // a-z
                    charCode === 45 || // hyphen (-)
                    charCode === 95 || // underscore (_)
                    charCode === 32)   // space
            ) {
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }


        function isNumberKey(evt) {
            var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : evt.keyCode;
            // Allow only numbers (0-9) and decimal point (.)
            if (charCode > 31 && (charCode < 48 || charCode > 57) && charCode !== 46) {
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }

        function removeSuffix(input) {
            // Remove " cent" if it exists
            if (input.value.endsWith(" cent")) {
                input.value = input.value.slice(0, -5); // Remove the last 5 characters
            }
        }

        function appendSuffix(input) {
            // Only append " cent" if the input is not empty and does not already end with " cent"
            if (input.value.trim() && !input.value.endsWith(" cent")) {
                input.value = input.value.trim() + " cent";
            }
        }

        function validateArea(input) {
            // Remove " cent" for validation
            const areaValue = input.value.replace(" cent", "").trim();

            // Check if the input is empty
            if (!areaValue) {
                alert("Please enter a valid area value.");
                input.focus();
                return false;
            }

            // Check if the input is a valid number
            const pattern = /^\d+(\.\d+)?$/; // Matches a valid number
            if (!pattern.test(areaValue)) {
                alert("Please enter a valid area (e.g., '100.50').");
                input.focus();
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

    </script>
</asp:Content>

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Linq;
using System.Web;

    public partial class Immovable_forms : System.Web.UI.Page

        protected void Page_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string Loaddata(string input)
        {
            string s = "";
            string requesterEmpCode = HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();
            string maininput = input + "~" + requesterEmpCode;
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "4", maininput);

            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString() + "~" + ds.Rows[0][1].ToString();
            }
            return s;

        }


        public class Dependent
        {
            public string DependentId { get; set; }
            public string DependentName { get; set; }
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static List<Dependent> LoadDependents(string input)
        {
            string requesterEmpCode = HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();

            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            List<Dependent> dependentInfo = new List<Dependent>();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            try
            {
                // Call the service to get dependent data
                ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "6", requesterEmpCode); // Adjust parameters as needed

                // Check if the DataTable has rows
                if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
                {
                    foreach (DataRow dr in ds.Rows)
                    {
                        dependentInfo.Add(new Dependent()
                        {
                            DependentId = dr[0].ToString(), // First column (ID or name)
                            DependentName = dr[1].ToString() // Second column (Description)
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Handle the exception (you might want to log it or return an error message)
                Console.WriteLine("An error occurred: " + ex.Message);
            }

            return dependentInfo;
        }


        public class State
        {
            public string StateId { get; set; }
            public string StateName { get; set; }
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static List<State> LoadState(string input)
        {
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            List<State> stateInfo = new List<State>();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            try
            {
                // Call the service to get dependent data
                ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "7", "0"); // Adjust parameters as needed

                // Check if the DataTable has rows
                if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
                {
                    foreach (DataRow dr in ds.Rows)
                    {
                        stateInfo.Add(new State()
                        {
                            StateId = dr[0].ToString(), // First column (ID or name)
                            StateName = dr[1].ToString() // Second column (Description)
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Handle the exception (you might want to log it or return an error message)
                Console.WriteLine("An error occurred: " + ex.Message);
            }

            return stateInfo;

        }


        public class District
        {
            public string DistrictId { get; set; }
            public string DistrictName { get; set; }
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static List<District> LoadDistrict(string input)
        {
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            List<District> districtInfo = new List<District>();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            try
            {
                // Call the service to get dependent data
                ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "8", input); // Adjust parameters as needed

                {
                    foreach (DataRow dr in ds.Rows)
                    {
                        districtInfo.Add(new District()
                        {
                            DistrictId = dr[0].ToString(), // First column (ID or name)
                            DistrictName = dr[1].ToString() // Second column (Description)
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Handle the exception (you might want to log it or return an error message)
                Console.WriteLine("An error occurred: " + ex.Message);
            }

            return districtInfo;

        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string inserttodb(string input)
        {
            string s = "";

            string data = input;
            // string fdat =  ;
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "9", data);
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();

            }
            return s;
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string insertnodb(string input)
        {
            string s = "";

            string data = input;
            // string fdat =  ;
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "10", data);
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();

            }
            return s;
        }




        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string checkpending(string input)
        {
            string s = "";
            string requesterEmpCode = HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(requesterEmpCode))
            {
                return "Error: User not logged in.";
            }

            try
            {
                using (HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient())
                {
                    DataTable ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "25", requesterEmpCode);
                    if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
                    {
                        s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Log the exception (ex) as needed
                return "Error: " + ex.Message;
            }

            return s;
        }

    }
