<asp:Content ID="Content1" ContentPlaceHolderID="head" runat="server">
    <script type="text/javascript">
        window.onload = function () {
            check2();
            dept_dtl();
            $("#ddl_emp").change(function () {
                var selectedValue = $(this).val(); // Get the selected value
                onDependentChange(selectedValue); // Call the function to handle the change
            });
        };
        function check2() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                url: "DEPT_AND_LIABILITY.aspx/checkpending", // Ensure this matches your server-side method name
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: "your_input_value" }), // Pass any required input
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    // Check the response
                    if (response.d === "111") { // Assuming response.d contains the returned value

                    } else {
                        alert("You have pending in Immovable page!");
                        window.location = "../ASSETS_AND_LIABILITY/Immovable_forms.aspx";
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error("Error details:", xhr.responseText); // Log error details
                    alert("Error: " + error);
                }
            });
        }
        function dept_dtl() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                url: "DEPT_AND_LIABILITY.aspx/LoadDependents", // Ensure this matches your server-side method name
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: "your_input_value" }), // Pass any required input
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    var result = response.d;
                    var ddlEmp = $("#ddl_emp");
                    ddlEmp.empty();

                    if (Array.isArray(result) && result.length > 1) {
                        result.forEach(function (item) {
                            ddlEmp.append($("<option></option>").val(item.DependentId).text(item.DependentName));
                        });
                    } else {
                        /// alert('You have successfully completed this page.');
                        window.location = "../Index/Index.aspx";
                    }
                },
                error: function (response) {
                    alert('Error: ' + response.responseText);
                }
            });
        }
        function onDependentChange(selectedValue) {
            document.getElementById('deptTable').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('radiodiv').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('creditordtldiv').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('debetordtldiv').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('rmkdiv').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('btndiv').style.display = 'none';
            var radioDiv = $("#radiodiv");
            if (selectedValue === "-1") {
                // Hide the main div if the selected value is -1
                radioDiv.hide();
                clearFormFields();
                clearTableRows();
            } else {
                // Show the main div for any other selected value
                radioDiv.show();
                document.getElementById('btndiv').style.display = 'block';
                $.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    url: "DEPT_AND_LIABILITY.aspx/Loaddata", // Ensure this matches your server-side method name
                    data: JSON.stringify({ input: selectedValue }), // Pass any required input
                    dataType: "json",
                    success: function (Result) {
                        var res = Result.d.split("~");
                        $('#dependantName').val(res[0]);
                        $('#position').val(res[1]);
                        clearTableRows();
                    },
                    error: function (response) {
                        alert('Error: ' + response.responseText);
                    }
                });
            }

        }

        function validateNumber(input) {
            if (isNaN(input.value) || input.value.trim() === "") {
                alert("Please enter a valid number.");
                input.value = "";
                input.focus();
            }
        }

        function clearFormFields() {
            document.getElementById('dependantName').value = "";
            document.getElementById('position').value = "";
            document.getElementById('credaddress').value = "";
            document.getElementById('credname').value = "";
            document.getElementById('dbt_liab').value = "0";
            document.getElementById('rmktxt').value = "";
            document.getElementById('debtamt').value = "";
        }

        function clearTableRows() {
            var tableBody = document.getElementById("deptTable");
            if (tableBody) {
                while (tableBody.rows.length > 0) {
                    tableBody.deleteRow(1);  // Always delete the first row until the table is empty
                }
            }
        }

        function toggledeptDiv() {
            var yesRadio = document.getElementById("1");
            var rmkdiv = document.getElementById("rmkdiv");
            var debetordtldiv = document.getElementById("debetordtldiv");
            var creditordtldiv = document.getElementById("creditordtldiv");
            var addDiv = document.getElementById("addbtn");
            var radioDiv = document.getElementById("radiodiv");
            document.getElementById('rmkdiv').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('debetordtldiv').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('creditordtldiv').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('addbtn').style.display = 'none';



            // Reset all fields to default values
            function resetFields() {
                // Reset text inputs
                var textInputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[type="text"]');
                textInputs.forEach(function (input) {
                    if (input.id !== 'dependantName' && input.id !== 'position') {
                        input.value = ''; // Clear the value
                    }
                });

                // Reset dropdowns to the first option
                var dropdowns = document.querySelectorAll('select');
                dropdowns.forEach(function (select) {
                    if (select.id !== 'ddl_emp') {
                        select.selectedIndex = 0; // Set to the first option
                    }
                });
            }

            if (yesRadio.checked) {
                creditordtldiv.style.display = "block"; // Show property div
                debetordtldiv.style.display = "block"; // Show property div
                rmkdiv.style.display = "block"; // Show property div
                addDiv.style.display = "block"; // Show property div
                clearTableRows();
                resetFields();
            } else {
                creditordtldiv.style.display = "none"; // Hide property div
                radioDiv.style.display = "block";
                debetordtldiv.style.display = "none"; // Hide property div
                rmkdiv.style.display = "none";// Show radio div
                addDiv.style.display = "none"; // Show property div
                resetFields();
            }
        }

        function onlyAlphabets(event, input) {
            var charCode = (event.which) ? event.which : event.keyCode;

            if (charCode === 8 || charCode === 9 || charCode === 13 || charCode === 32 || // backspace, tab, enter, space
                (charCode >= 65 && charCode <= 90) || // A-Z
                (charCode >= 97 && charCode <= 122)) { // a-z
                return true; // Allow the character
            }

            // Prevent the default action for any other key
            return false;
        }

        function add_prop() {
            // Check if the dependent dropdown is selected
            var dependentDropdown = document.getElementById('ddl_emp');
            if (dependentDropdown.value === '0') {
                alert("Please select a dependent.");
                return; // Exit the function if the condition is not met
            }
            var yesRadio = document.getElementById("1");
            var noRadio = document.getElementById("2"); // Assuming you have a "No" radio button with id "2"
            if (!yesRadio.checked && !noRadio.checked) {
                alert("Please select any radio button.");
                return; // Exit the function if the condition is not met
            }


            var remark = document.getElementById('rmktxt').value;
            var amount = document.getElementById('debtamt').value;
            var address = document.getElementById('credaddress').value;
            var name = document.getElementById('credname').value;
            if (remark.startsWith(' ') || amount.startsWith(' ') || address.startsWith(' ') || name.startsWith(' ')) {
                alert("Fields cannot start with a space.");
                return; // Exit the function if any field starts with a space
            }
            if (!remark || !amount || !address || !name) {
                alert("Please fill in all fields."); // Show an alert if any field is empty
                return;
            }
            // Check if the radio button for property ownership is "Yes"
            var liab = document.getElementById('dbt_liab');
            if (yesRadio.checked && liab.value === '0') {
                alert("Please select Debt or Liability.");
                return; // Exit the function if the condition is not met
            }

            // Get the selected option text from the debt/liability dropdown
            var selectedOption = liab.options[liab.selectedIndex];
            var selectedText = selectedOption.text;
            // Add the property to the table
            var table = document.getElementById("deptTable");
            var newRow = table.insertRow(-1);
            newRow.insertCell(0).innerText = name;
            newRow.insertCell(1).innerText = address;
            newRow.insertCell(2).innerText = selectedText;
            newRow.insertCell(3).innerText = amount;
            newRow.insertCell(4).innerText = remark;

            // Add a new cell for the delete icon
            var deleteCell = newRow.insertCell(5);
            var deleteIcon = document.createElement("span");
            deleteIcon.className = "glyphicon glyphicon-trash"; // Bootstrap glyphicon for trash
            deleteIcon.style.cursor = "pointer"; // Change cursor to pointer
            deleteIcon.onclick = function () {
                deleteRow(newRow);
            };
            deleteCell.appendChild(deleteIcon);

            table.style.display = "table"; // Ensure the table is displayed
            clearFormFields2();

            // If all checks pass, show a success message
            alert("Row Added.");
        }

        function deleteRow(row) {
            var table = document.getElementById("deptTable");
            table.deleteRow(row.rowIndex); // Delete the row from the table
            alert("Row Deleted."); // Optional: Alert the user

            // Check if the table should be hidden
            if (table.rows.length <= 1) {
                table.style.display = "none"; // Hide the table if no rows are left
            }
        }

        function clearFormFields2() {
            document.getElementById('credaddress').value = "";
            document.getElementById('credname').value = "";
            document.getElementById('dbt_liab').value = "0";
            document.getElementById('rmktxt').value = "";
            document.getElementById('debtamt').value = "";
        }

        function insert() {
            var tableBody = document.getElementById("deptTable");
            var radioYes = document.getElementById("1");
            var radioNo = document.getElementById("2");
            if (radioYes.checked) {
                // Check if there are any rows in the table body
                if (tableBody.rows.length === 1) {
                    alert("No rows have been inserted. Please add at least one row before proceeding.");
                    return; // Exit the function to prevent the insert operation
                }
                else {
                    insrttbldb();
                }
            }
            else if (radioNo.checked)
                // If "No" is selected 
                insertnoradio();
            else {
                alert("Please select at least one radio button before proceeding.");
            }
        }

        function insrttbldb() {
            var ddlEmp = document.getElementById('ddl_emp');

            // Get the selected value
            var selecteddepValue = ddlEmp.value;

            // Get the selected text
            var selecteddepText = ddlEmp.options[ddlEmp.selectedIndex].text;
            var values = selecteddepText.split('-'); // Use split method directly on the string
            var dependent = values[0].trim(); // Use trim() to remove any leading/trailing whitespace

            var dataToInsert = ""; // Initialize an empty string to hold the concatenated data

            // Ensure the table is visible before trying to access its rows
            var table = $("#deptTable");
            if (table.is(":visible")) {
                // Loop through each row starting from index 1 to skip the header
                for (var j = 1; j < table.find("tr").length; j++) {
                    // Get the current row
                    var row = table.find("tr").eq(j);

                    // Get all cells in the current row
                    var cells = row.find("td"); // Assuming you are using <td> for table data cells

                    // Check if the row has the expected number of cells
                    if (cells.length < 5) { // Adjusted to 5 since you have 5 data columns
                        console.warn("Row " + j + " does not have enough cells.");
                        continue; // Skip this row if it doesn't have enough cells
                    }

                    // Extract data from each cell
                    var name = $(cells[0]).text();
                    var add = $(cells[1]).text();
                    var nature = $(cells[2]).text();
                    var amount = $(cells[3]).text();
                    var remark = $(cells[4]).text();

                    // Concatenate the data into the desired format
                    dataToInsert += name + "*" + add + "*" + nature + "*" + amount + "*" + remark + "!";
                }
            } else {
                console.warn("Table is not visible.");
            }

            // Prepare the final data string
            var data = dependent + "#" + selecteddepValue + "#" + dataToInsert;
            //alert(data);
            insrtajx(data);
        }

        function insrtajx(data) {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "DEPT_AND_LIABILITY.aspx/inserttodb",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: data }), // Use JSON.stringify
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    // Check the response
                    if (response.d === "111") { // Assuming response.d contains the returned value
                        checkPdfRequired();
                       // alert("Data inserted successfully!");
                        //location.reload();
                    } else {
                        alert("Data is already inserted!");
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error("Error details:", xhr.responseText); // Log error details
                    alert("Error: " + error);
                }
            });
        }

        function checkPdfRequired() {

            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "DEPT_AND_LIABILITY.aspx/checkPdfRequired",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                data: JSON.stringify({}),
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {

                    //if (response.d === "111") {
                    //    GeneratePdf();
                    //    //alert("ALL DEPENDENTS COMPLETED");
                    //}
                    if (response.d === "111") {
                        $("#pdfLoading").show();

                        $("#Button2").prop("disabled", true);

                        GeneratePdf();
                    }
                    else {

                        alert("DEPENDENTS STILL PENDING");
                        location.reload();
                        return;
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {

                    $("#pdfLoading").hide();
                    $("#Button2").prop("disabled", false);

                    alert("Error while generating PDF.");
                }
            });
        }

        function GeneratePdf() {

            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "DEPT_AND_LIABILITY.aspx/GeneratePdf",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                data: JSON.stringify({}),
                dataType: "json",
                //success: function (response) {
                //    alert("Asset & Liability PDF generated successfully.");
                //    window.location = "../Index/Index.aspx";
                //},
                success: function (response) {

                    $("#pdfLoading").hide();

                    alert("Assets & Liabilities submitted successfully.");

                    window.location = "../Index/Index.aspx";
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    alert(error);
                }
            });
        }

        function insertnoradio() {
            var ddlEmp = document.getElementById('ddl_emp');
            // Get the selected value
            var selecteddepValue = ddlEmp.value;

            // Get the selected text
            var selecteddepText = ddlEmp.options[ddlEmp.selectedIndex].text;
            var values = selecteddepText.split('-'); // Use split method directly on the string
            var dependent = values[0].trim(); // Use trim() to remove any leading/trailing whitespace
            data = dependent + "~" + selecteddepValue;
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "DEPT_AND_LIABILITY.aspx/insertnodb",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: data }), // Use JSON.stringify
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    // Check the response
                    if (response.d === "111") { // Assuming response.d contains the returned value
                        checkPdfRequired();
                        //alert("Data inserted successfully!");
                        //location.reload();
                    } else {
                        alert("Data is already inserted!");
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error("Error details:", xhr.responseText); // Log error details
                    alert("Error: " + error);
                }
            });
        }

        function exit() {
            window.location = "../Index/Index.aspx";
        }
    </script>

                        <div class="form-group">
                            <label class="col-md-2 cntr-text">NAME</label>
                            <div class="col-md-4">
                                <input type="text" name="regular" class="form-control" id="credname" maxlength="30"
                                    onblur="leftTrim(this)"
                                    onkeypress="return onlyAlphabets(event, this);"
                                    onkeyup="this.value = this.value.toUpperCase()" />
                            </div>
                            <label class="col-md-2 cntr-text">ADDRESS</label>
                            <div class="col-md-4">
                                <input type="text" name="regular" class="form-control" id="credaddress" maxlength="30" onblur="leftTrim(this)" onkeyup="this.value = this.value.toUpperCase()" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <%--///////next div/////////--%>

            <div class="form-group" id="debetordtldiv" style="display: none;">
                <div class="col-md-12">
                    <div class="widget-header">
                        <h4><i class="icon-reorder"></i>NATURE OF DEBTOR  <span class="required">*</span></h4>

                                <option value="2">Liability </option>
                            </select>

                        <label class="col-md-2 cntr-text">Amount</label>
                        <div class="col-md-4">
                            <input type="text" name="regular" class="form-control" maxlength="30" id="debtamt" onblur="validateNumber(this);" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <%--///////next div/////////--%>

            <div class="form-group" id="rmkdiv" style="display: none;">
                <div class="col-md-12">
                    <div class="widget-header">
                        <h4><i class="icon-reorder"></i>REMARKS<span class="required">*</span></h4>
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label class="col-md-2 cntr-text">REMARK</label>
                        <div class="col-md-4">
                            <textarea name="regular" class="form-control" maxlength="30" id="rmktxt" onblur="leftTrim(this)" onkeyup="this.value = this.value.toUpperCase()"></textarea>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="form-group align-center" id="addbtn" style="display: none;">
                        <input id="btn_prope" type="button" value="ADD " class="btn btn-primary" style="border-radius: 10%; background-color: #067006" onclick="add_prop();" />
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <%--///////next div/////////--%>

            <div id="debtTableContainer">
                <table id="deptTable" class="table table-bordered" style="display: none;">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>Name of Creditor</th>
                            <th>Address of Creditor</th>

            <%--///////next div/////////--%>

            <div class="form-group align-center" id="btndiv" style="display: none;">
                <input id="Button2" type="button" value="CONFIRM " class="btn btn-success" style="border-radius: 15%; background-color: #067006" onclick="insert();" />
                <input id="Button3" type="button" value="EXIT " class="btn btn-danger" style="border-radius: 15%; background-color: #e30e0e" onclick="exit();" />
            </div>
        </div>
        <div id="pdfLoading"
            style="display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; background: rgba(0,0,0,.4); z-index: 99999;">

            <div style="position: absolute; top: 50%; left: 50%; transform: translate(-50%,-50%); background: white; padding: 20px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center;">

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Linq;
using System.Web;
using System.Web.Services;
using iTextSharp.text;
using iTextSharp.text.pdf;
using System.IO;

namespace Ma_AppSuite.ASSETS_AND_LIABILITY
{
    public partial class DEPT_AND_LIABILITY : System.Web.UI.Page
    {
        protected void Page_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        public class Dependent
        {
            public string DependentId { get; set; }
            public string DependentName { get; set; }
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static List<Dependent> LoadDependents(string input)
        {
            string requesterEmpCode = HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();

            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            List<Dependent> dependentInfo = new List<Dependent>();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            try
            {
                // Call the service to get dependent data
                ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "12", requesterEmpCode); // Adjust parameters as needed

                // Check if the DataTable has rows
                if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
                {
                    foreach (DataRow dr in ds.Rows)
                    {
                        dependentInfo.Add(new Dependent()
                        {
                            DependentId = dr[0].ToString(), // First column (ID or name)
                            DependentName = dr[1].ToString() // Second column (Description)
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Handle the exception (you might want to log it or return an error message)
                Console.WriteLine("An error occurred: " + ex.Message);
            }

            return dependentInfo;
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string Loaddata(string input)
        {
            string s = "";
            string requesterEmpCode = HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();
            string maininput = input + "~" + requesterEmpCode;
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "4", maininput);

            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString() + "~" + ds.Rows[0][1].ToString();
            }
            return s;
        }


        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string inserttodb(string input)
        {
            string s = "";

            string data = input;
            // string fdat =  ;
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "14", data);
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();

            }
            return s;
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string insertnodb(string input)
        {
            string s = "";

            string data = input;
            // string fdat =  ;
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "13", data);
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();

            }
            return s;
        }
        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string checkpending(string input)
        {
            string s = "";
            string requesterEmpCode = HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(requesterEmpCode))
            {
                return "Error: User not logged in.";
            }

            try
            {
                using (HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient())
                {
                    DataTable ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "26", requesterEmpCode);
                    if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
                    {
                        s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Log the exception (ex) as needed
                return "Error: " + ex.Message;
            }

            return s;
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string checkPdfRequired()
        {
            string s = "";
            string requesterEmpCode =
                HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();

            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj =
                new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();

            DataTable ds =
                obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "27", requesterEmpCode);

            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();
            }

            return s;
        }
        
        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string GeneratePdf()
        {
            string empCode = HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(empCode))
                throw new Exception("User session expired. Please log in again.");

            using (HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient())
            {
                // 1. Fetch existing data using your current stored procedure flags
                //DataTable dtMovable = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "18", empCode);
                //DataTable dtInvestment = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "19", empCode);
                //DataTable dtOtherAsset = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "20", empCode);
                //DataTable dtImmovable = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "21", empCode);
                //DataTable dtDebt = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "22", empCode);
                //DataTable dtVehicle = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "23", empCode);
                //DataTable dtJewellery = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "15", empCode);
                //DataTable dtFamily = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "28", empCode);
                DataTable dtSeq = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "29", empCode);
                string seqId = "";

                if (dtSeq.Rows.Count > 0)
                    seqId = dtSeq.Rows[0]["SEQ_ID"].ToString();
                //string pdfSeqId = seqId;
                DataTable dtMovable =
                    obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "33", seqId);

                DataTable dtInvestment =
                    obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "34", seqId);

                DataTable dtOtherAsset =
                    obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "35", seqId);

                DataTable dtImmovable =
                    obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "36", seqId);

                DataTable dtDebt =
                    obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "37", seqId);

                DataTable dtVehicle =
                    obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "38", seqId);

                DataTable dtJewellery =
                    obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "32", seqId);

                DataTable dtFamily = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "28", empCode);


                // 2. Create the PDF file
                string folderPath = HttpContext.Current.Server.MapPath("~/PDF/");

                if (!Directory.Exists(folderPath))
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(folderPath);

                string fileName = empCode + "_ASSETLIABILITY_" +
                                  DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmss") + ".pdf";

                string filePath = Path.Combine(folderPath, fileName);

                Document document = new Document(
                    PageSize.A4, 30, 30, 30, 30);


                FileStream fs = new FileStream(
                    filePath,
                    FileMode.Create,
                    FileAccess.Write);

                try
                {
                    PdfWriter writer =
                        PdfWriter.GetInstance(document, fs);

                    string watermarkPath =
                        HttpContext.Current.Server.MapPath(
                        "~/Resignation/images/1000296037.png");

                    writer.PageEvent =
                        new PdfPageEvents(watermarkPath);

                    document.Open();

                    // 3. Fonts
                    Font titleFont = FontFactory.GetFont(
                            FontFactory.TIMES_BOLD, 16);

                        Font sectionFont = FontFactory.GetFont(
                            FontFactory.TIMES_BOLD, 13);

                        Font headerFont = FontFactory.GetFont(
                            FontFactory.TIMES_BOLD, 9);

                        Font bodyFont = FontFactory.GetFont(
                            FontFactory.TIMES_ROMAN, 9);

                        // 4. Reusable table-cell formatting
                        Func<string, PdfPCell> headerCell = delegate (string text)
                        {
                            return new PdfPCell(new Phrase(text ?? "", headerFont))
                            {
                                Padding = 5,
                                VerticalAlignment = Element.ALIGN_MIDDLE,
                                HorizontalAlignment = Element.ALIGN_LEFT,
                                BorderWidth = 0.8f
                            };
                        };

                        Func<string, PdfPCell> dataCell = delegate (string text)
                        {
                            return new PdfPCell(new Phrase(
                                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text) ? "NIL" : text,
                                bodyFont))
                            {
                                Padding = 5,
                                VerticalAlignment = Element.ALIGN_MIDDLE,
                                HorizontalAlignment = Element.ALIGN_LEFT,
                                BorderWidth = 0.8f
                            };
                        };

                        // 5. PAGE 1 - Employee, spouse and dependents
                        Paragraph title = new Paragraph(
                            "Assets and Liabilities", titleFont);

                        title.Alignment = Element.ALIGN_CENTER;
                        title.SpacingAfter = 25f;
                        document.Add(title);

                        Paragraph subTitle = new Paragraph(
                            "Details of Employee, spouse and dependents",
                            sectionFont);

                        subTitle.Alignment = Element.ALIGN_CENTER;
                        subTitle.SpacingAfter = 18f;
                        document.Add(subTitle);

                        PdfPTable empTable = new PdfPTable(4);
                        empTable.WidthPercentage = 100;
                        empTable.SetWidths(new float[] { 10, 35, 25, 30 });
                        empTable.HeaderRows = 1;

                        empTable.AddCell(headerCell("SL NO."));
                        empTable.AddCell(headerCell("Name"));
                        empTable.AddCell(headerCell("Position held, if any"));
                        empTable.AddCell(headerCell(
                            "Whether form being filed by him/her separately"));

                        int empSlNo = 1;

                        foreach (DataRow dr in dtFamily.Rows)
                        {
                            empTable.AddCell(dataCell(empSlNo.ToString()));

                            empTable.AddCell(dataCell(
                                dr["SPOUSE_AND_DEPEN_NAME"].ToString()));

                            empTable.AddCell(dataCell(
                                dr["POSITION"].ToString()));

                            empTable.AddCell(dataCell(
                                dr["WHETHER_FORM_BEING_FILED_BY_HIM_HER"].ToString() == "1"
                                ? "Yes" : "No"));

                            empSlNo++;
                        }

                        document.Add(empTable);

                        // 6. PAGE 2 - Movable property
                        document.NewPage();

                        Paragraph movableTitle = new Paragraph(
                            "Statement of movable property", sectionFont);

                        movableTitle.Alignment = Element.ALIGN_CENTER;
                        movableTitle.SpacingAfter = 18f;
                        document.Add(movableTitle);

                        PdfPTable movableForm = new PdfPTable(3);
                        movableForm.WidthPercentage = 100;
                        movableForm.SetWidths(new float[] { 10, 60, 30 });
                        movableForm.HeaderRows = 1;

                        movableForm.AddCell(headerCell("SL NO."));
                        movableForm.AddCell(headerCell("Description"));
                        movableForm.AddCell(headerCell("Remark, if any"));

                        // Cash and bank balance
                        string cashBalance = "NIL";

                        if (dtMovable.Rows.Count > 0)
                        {
                            cashBalance = string.Join(", ",
                                dtMovable.AsEnumerable().Select(x =>
                                    x["DEPENDANT"].ToString() + " : " +
                                    (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["CASH_AND_BANKBALANCE"].ToString()) ||
                                     x["CASH_AND_BANKBALANCE"].ToString() == "0"
                                        ? "NIL"
                                        : x["CASH_AND_BANKBALANCE"].ToString())));
                        }

                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell("i."));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell("Cash and bank balance"));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(cashBalance));

                        // Investments
                        string investmentText = "NIL";

                        if (dtInvestment.Rows.Count > 0)
                        {
                            investmentText = string.Join(", ",
                                dtInvestment.AsEnumerable().Select(x =>
                                    x["INVESTMENT"].ToString() + " : " +
                                    (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["AMOUNT"].ToString()) ||
                                     x["AMOUNT"].ToString() == "0"
                                        ? "NIL"
                                        : x["AMOUNT"].ToString())));
                        }

                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell("ii."));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                            "Insurance (premia paid); Fixed / Recurring Deposits; " +
                            "Shares / Bonds; Mutual Funds; Pension Scheme / " +
                            "Provident Fund; Other investments, if any"));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(investmentText));

                        // Personal loans
                        string personalLoan = "NIL";

                        if (dtMovable.Rows.Count > 0)
                        {
                            personalLoan = string.Join(", ",
                                dtMovable.AsEnumerable().Select(x =>
                                    x["DEPENDANT"].ToString() + " : " +
                                    (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["PERSONAL_LOAN"].ToString()) ||
                                     x["PERSONAL_LOAN"].ToString() == "0"
                                        ? "NIL"
                                        : x["PERSONAL_LOAN"].ToString())));
                        }

                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell("iii."));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                            "Personal loans / advances given to any person or entity, " +
                            "including firms, companies or trusts, and other receivables"));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(personalLoan));

                        // Vehicles
                        string vehicleText = "NIL";

                        if (dtVehicle.Rows.Count > 0)
                        {
                            vehicleText = string.Join(", ",
                                dtVehicle.AsEnumerable().Select(x =>
                                {
                                    string vehicle = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["VEHICLES"].ToString())
                                        ? "NIL" : x["VEHICLES"].ToString();

                                    string regNo = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["REGISTRATION_NUMBER"].ToString())
                                        ? "NIL" : x["REGISTRATION_NUMBER"].ToString();

                                    string year = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["YEAR_OF_PURCHASE"].ToString()) ||
                                        x["YEAR_OF_PURCHASE"].ToString() == "0"
                                        ? "NIL" : x["YEAR_OF_PURCHASE"].ToString();

                                    return vehicle + " - " + regNo + " - " + year;
                                }));
                        }

                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell("iv."));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                            "Motor vehicles (make, registration number, " +
                            "year of purchase and amount paid)"));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(vehicleText));

                        // Jewellery
                        string jewelleryText = "NIL";

                        if (dtJewellery.Rows.Count > 0)
                        {
                            jewelleryText = string.Join(", ",
                                dtJewellery.AsEnumerable().Select(x =>
                                    (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["JEWELS"].ToString())
                                        ? "NIL" : x["JEWELS"].ToString()) +
                                    " - " +
                                    (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["WEIGHT"].ToString()) ||
                                     x["WEIGHT"].ToString() == "0"
                                        ? "NIL" : x["WEIGHT"].ToString())));
                        }

                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell("v."));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                            "Jewellery: gold, silver, precious metals, " +
                            "precious stones and composite items; " +
                            "indicate approximate weight and value"));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(jewelleryText));

                        // Other assets
                        string otherAssetText = "NIL";

                        if (dtOtherAsset.Rows.Count > 0)
                        {
                            otherAssetText = string.Join(", ",
                                dtOtherAsset.AsEnumerable().Select(x =>
                                    x["OTHER_ASSETS"].ToString() + " : " +
                                    (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                        x["VALUE"].ToString()) ||
                                     x["VALUE"].ToString() == "0"
                                        ? "NIL" : x["VALUE"].ToString())));
                        }

                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell("vi."));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                            "Other movable assets: furniture, fixtures, antiques, " +
                            "paintings, electronic equipment and others"));
                        movableForm.AddCell(dataCell(otherAssetText));

                        document.Add(movableForm);

                        // 7. PAGE 3 - Immovable property
                        document.NewPage();

                        Paragraph immovableTitle = new Paragraph(
                            "Statement of immovable property", sectionFont);

                        immovableTitle.Alignment = Element.ALIGN_CENTER;
                        immovableTitle.SpacingAfter = 18f;
                        document.Add(immovableTitle);

                        PdfPTable immovableForm = new PdfPTable(3);
                        immovableForm.WidthPercentage = 100;
                        immovableForm.SetWidths(new float[] { 10, 55, 35 });
                        immovableForm.HeaderRows = 1;

                        immovableForm.AddCell(headerCell("SL NO."));
                        immovableForm.AddCell(headerCell("Description"));
                        immovableForm.AddCell(headerCell("Details"));

                        int immSlNo = 1;

                        foreach (DataRow imm in dtImmovable.Rows)
                        {
                            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                imm["PROPERTY_TYPE"].ToString()))
                                continue;

                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(immSlNo++.ToString()));
                            immSlNo++;
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                                "Property of " + imm["DEPENDANT"].ToString()));
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                                imm["PROPERTY_TYPE"].ToString()));

                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(immSlNo++.ToString()));
                            immSlNo++;
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell("Details"));
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                                imm["DETAILS"].ToString()));

                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(immSlNo++.ToString()));
                            immSlNo++;
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell("Precise location"));
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                                (imm["STATE_NAME"].ToString() + ", " +
                                 imm["DISTRICT_NAME"].ToString()).Trim(' ', ',')));

                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(immSlNo++.ToString()));
                            immSlNo++;
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell("Area of land"));
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                                imm["TOTAL_AREA"].ToString()));

                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(immSlNo++.ToString()));
                            immSlNo++;
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell("Present value"));
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                                imm["PRESENT_VALUE"].ToString()));

                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(immSlNo++.ToString()));
                            immSlNo++;
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell("Remark"));
                            immovableForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                                imm["REMARK"].ToString()));
                        }

                        document.Add(immovableForm);

                        // 8. PAGE 4 - Debts and other liabilities
                        document.NewPage();

                        Paragraph debtTitle = new Paragraph(
                            "Statement of Debts and Other Liabilities",
                            sectionFont);

                        debtTitle.Alignment = Element.ALIGN_CENTER;
                        debtTitle.SpacingAfter = 18f;
                        document.Add(debtTitle);

                        PdfPTable debtForm = new PdfPTable(5);
                        debtForm.WidthPercentage = 100;
                        debtForm.SetWidths(new float[] { 8, 20, 27, 27, 18 });
                        debtForm.HeaderRows = 1;

                        debtForm.AddCell(headerCell("SI NO."));
                        debtForm.AddCell(headerCell("Debtor"));
                        debtForm.AddCell(headerCell(
                            "Name and address of creditor"));
                        debtForm.AddCell(headerCell(
                            "Nature of debt / liability and amount"));
                        debtForm.AddCell(headerCell("Remark"));

                        int slNo = 1;

                        if (dtDebt.Rows.Count > 0)
                        {
                            foreach (DataRow dr in dtDebt.Rows)
                            {
                                string name = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                    dr["NAME"].ToString())
                                    ? "NIL" : dr["NAME"].ToString();

                                string address = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                    dr["ADDRESS"].ToString())
                                    ? "NIL" : dr["ADDRESS"].ToString();

                                string nature = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                    dr["NATURE"].ToString())
                                    ? "NIL" : dr["NATURE"].ToString();

                                string amount = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                    dr["AMOUNT"].ToString())
                                    ? "NIL" : dr["AMOUNT"].ToString();

                                string remark = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                    dr["REMARK"].ToString())
                                    ? "NIL" : dr["REMARK"].ToString();

                                string creditorInfo =
                                    (name == "NIL" && address == "NIL")
                                    ? "NIL"
                                    : name + "\n" + address;

                                string debtInfo =
                                    (nature == "NIL" && amount == "NIL")
                                    ? "NIL"
                                    : nature + "\nAmount: " + amount;

                                debtForm.AddCell(dataCell(slNo.ToString()));
                                debtForm.AddCell(dataCell(
                                    dr["DEPENDANT"].ToString()));
                                debtForm.AddCell(dataCell(creditorInfo));
                                debtForm.AddCell(dataCell(debtInfo));
                                debtForm.AddCell(dataCell(remark));

                                slNo++;
                            }
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            debtForm.AddCell(dataCell("1"));
                            debtForm.AddCell(dataCell("NIL"));
                            debtForm.AddCell(dataCell("NIL"));
                            debtForm.AddCell(dataCell("NIL"));
                            debtForm.AddCell(dataCell("NIL"));
                        }

                        document.Add(debtForm);
                    }
                    finally
                    {
                        if (document.IsOpen())
                            document.Close();
                        fs.Dispose();
                    }

            
                

                // 9. Upload the generated PDF using your existing service
                byte[] pdfBytes = File.ReadAllBytes(filePath);

                string uploadData =
                    seqId + "µ" + empCode + "µ" + fileName;

                string result = obj.EnrolDocumentUpload(
                    "286", uploadData, pdfBytes);

                return fileName;
            }

        }
    }
    public class PdfPageEvents : PdfPageEventHelper
    {
        private readonly string _watermarkImagePath;

        public PdfPageEvents(string watermarkImagePath)
        {
            _watermarkImagePath = watermarkImagePath;
        }

        public override void OnEndPage(
            PdfWriter writer,
            Document document)
        {
            PdfContentByte under =
                writer.DirectContentUnder;

            Image watermark =
                Image.GetInstance(_watermarkImagePath);

            watermark.ScalePercent(25f);

            watermark.SetAbsolutePosition(
                (document.PageSize.Width - watermark.ScaledWidth) / 2,
                (document.PageSize.Height - watermark.ScaledHeight) / 2);

            PdfGState state = new PdfGState();
            state.FillOpacity = 0.05f;

            under.SaveState();
            under.SetGState(state);
            under.AddImage(watermark);
            under.RestoreState();
        }
    }
}

        function access() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "asset_liability_report1.aspx/access",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                data: JSON.stringify({ input: null }), // Use null instead of "null"
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (response) {
                    // Check the response
                    if (response.d === "111") { // Assuming response.d contains the returned value
                        // User is authorized, you can proceed with your logic here
                    } else {
                        alert("You are not authorized to view this page!");
                        window.location = "../index/index.aspx";
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error("Error details:", xhr.responseText); // Log error details
                    alert("Error: " + error);
                }
            });
        }

            window.location = "../index/index.aspx";

        function emp_load_fn() {

            var count = $("[id*=txt_empcd]").val();


            if (count == '') {
                alert("Please Enter Employee Code");
                return false;
            }

            $.ajax({

                type: "POST",
                url: "asset_liability_report1.aspx/emp_load_mt",
                data: "{input:'" + count + "'}",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (Result) {

                    Res = Result.d;
                    if (Res === null || Res.length === 0) {
                        alert("No Employee data found.");
                    }
                    else {

                        table_load2(Res);
                    }

                },

                error: function () {

                    alert('Error!');

                }
            });

            $.ajax({

                type: "POST",
                url: "asset_liability_report1.aspx/get_emp",

        function isNumberKey(evt, element) {
            var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : evt.keyCode;

            // Allow: backspace, tab, enter, and numbers
            if (charCode === 8 || charCode === 9 || charCode === 13 || (charCode >= 48 && charCode <= 57)) {
                return true;
            }

            return false;
        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string access(string input)
        {
            string s = "";

            string requesterEmpCode = HttpContext.Current.Session["username"]?.ToString();
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();

            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "24", requesterEmpCode);
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();

            }
            return s;
        }
        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string emp_load_mt(string input)
        {
            string tblString = "";
            string s = "";
            string final = input;

            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();
            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "17", final);
            s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                foreach (DataRow dr in ds.Rows)
                {
                    tblString = tblString
                        + dr[0] + "^"
                        + dr[1] + "^"
                        + dr[2] + "^"
                        + dr[3] + "^"
                        + dr[4] + "^"
                        + "Θ";
                }
            }

            return tblString.ToString();


        }

        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string get_emp(string input)
        {
            string tblString = "";
            string final = input;

            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "15", final);

            // Check if the DataTable is null or has no rows
            if (ds == null || ds.Rows.Count == 0)
            {
                // If ds is null or empty, create a null row representation with column names
                tblString = "null^null^null^null^Θ"; // Adjust column names as needed
            }
            else
            {
                foreach (DataRow dr in ds.Rows)
                {
                    tblString += dr[0] + "^"
                               + dr[1] + "^"
                               + dr[2] + "^"
                               + dr[3] + "^"
                               + "Θ";
                }
            }

            return tblString;
        }

        protected void BtnExport_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();
            DataTable ds1 = new DataTable();
            DataTable ds2 = new DataTable();
            DataTable ds3 = new DataTable();
            DataTable ds4 = new DataTable();
            DataTable ds5 = new DataTable();
            DataTable ds6 = new DataTable();
            DataTable ds7 = new DataTable();




            if (txt_empcd.Text == "")
            {

                Response.Write("<script language=javascript>alert('Please enter Employee Code');</script>");

            }
            else
            {

                var fdt = Convert.ToString(txt_empcd.Text);


                string input = fdt.ToString();

                ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "17", input);
                ds1 = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "15", input);

                ds2 = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "18", input);
                ds3 = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "19", input);
                ds4 = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "20", input);
                ds5 = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "21", input);
                ds6 = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "22", input);
                ds7 = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "23", input);




                //string htmlString = "";


                if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
                {
                    DataTableToHTMLTable(ds);



                    DateTime dt = DateTime.Now;



                    string date = dt.ToString("MM/dd/yyyy:HH:mm:ss");




                    string fname = "ASSETS AND LIABILITIES REPORT";
                    System.IO.StringWriter tw = new System.IO.StringWriter();
                    System.Web.UI.HtmlTextWriter hw = new System.Web.UI.HtmlTextWriter(tw);
                    DataGrid dgGrid = new DataGrid();
                    dgGrid.Caption = "Employee Details";
                    dgGrid.DataSource = ds;
                    dgGrid.DataBind();




                    dgGrid.RenderControl(hw);

                    //page 4
                    DataGrid dgGrid3 = new DataGrid();
                    dgGrid3.Caption = "Investment Details";
                    dgGrid3.DataSource = ds3;
                    dgGrid3.DataBind();




                    dgGrid3.RenderControl(hw);


                    // page 2
                    DataGrid dgGrid1 = new DataGrid();
                    dgGrid1.Caption = "Jewellery Details";
                    dgGrid1.DataSource = ds1;
                    dgGrid1.DataBind();






                    dgGrid1.RenderControl(hw);




                    // page 3



                    DataGrid dgGrid2 = new DataGrid();
                    dgGrid2.Caption = "Movable Details";
                    dgGrid2.DataSource = ds2;
                    dgGrid2.DataBind();




                    dgGrid2.RenderControl(hw);





                    DataGrid dgGrid4 = new DataGrid();
                    dgGrid4.Caption = "Other Asset Details";
                    dgGrid4.DataSource = ds4;
                    dgGrid4.DataBind();




                    dgGrid4.RenderControl(hw);



                    DataGrid dgGrid5 = new DataGrid();
                    dgGrid5.Caption = "Immovable Details";
                    dgGrid5.DataSource = ds5;
                    dgGrid5.DataBind();




                    dgGrid5.RenderControl(hw);


                    DataGrid dgGrid6 = new DataGrid();
                    dgGrid6.Caption = "Dept and Liabilities Details";
                    dgGrid6.DataSource = ds6;
                    dgGrid6.DataBind();




                    dgGrid6.RenderControl(hw);

                    DataGrid dgGrid7 = new DataGrid();
                    dgGrid7.Caption = "Vehicle Details";
                    dgGrid7.DataSource = ds7;
                    dgGrid7.DataBind();




                    dgGrid7.RenderControl(hw);





                    Response.ContentType = "application/vnd.ms-excel";
                    Response.AppendHeader("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=" + fname.ToString() + date + ".xls");
                    this.EnableViewState = false;
                    Response.Write(tw.ToString());
                    Response.End();



                }
            }
        }
        public string DataTableToHTMLTable(DataTable inTable)
        {
            System.Text.StringBuilder dString = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            dString.Append("<table id='example' class='display' cellpadding='0' cellspacing='0' border='0'>");
            dString.Append(GetHeader(inTable));
            dString.Append(GetBody(inTable));
            dString.Append("</table>");
            //MyTable.InnerHtml = dString.ToString();
            return "";
        }
        private string GetHeader(DataTable dTable)
        {
            System.Text.StringBuilder dString = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            dString.Append("<thead><tr>");
            foreach (DataColumn dColumn in dTable.Columns)
            {
                dString.AppendFormat("<th>{0}</th>", dColumn.ColumnName);
            }
            dString.Append("</tr></thead>");
            return dString.ToString();
        }
        private string GetBody(DataTable dTable)
        {
            System.Text.StringBuilder dString = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            dString.Append("<tbody>");
            foreach (DataRow dRow in dTable.Rows)
            {
                dString.Append("<tr class='odd_gradeX'>");
                for (int dCount = 0; dCount < dTable.Columns.Count; dCount++)
                {
                    dString.AppendFormat("<td style=text-align:left>{0}</td>", dRow[dCount]);
                }
                dString.Append("</tr>");
            }
            dString.Append("</tbody>");
            return dString.ToString();
        }
    }

        function emp_load_fn() {

           // var count = $("[id*=txt_empcd]").val();

             var EMP = $("[id*=txt_session]").val();

            //alert(EMP);

         

            $.ajax({

                type: "POST",
                url: "Asset_And_Liability_Individual_Report.aspx/emp_load_mt",
                data: "{input:'" + EMP + "'}",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (Result) {

                    Res = Result.d;
                   
                    table_load2(Res);
                },

                error: function () {

                    alert('Error!');

                }
            });

            $.ajax({

                type: "POST",
                url: "Asset_And_Liability_Individual_Report.aspx/get_emp",
                data: "{input:'" + EMP + "'}",

                dataType: "json",

                    Res = Result.d;
                    table_load(Res);
                },

                    alert('Error!');

                }
            });

                url: "Asset_And_Liability_Individual_Report.aspx/get_VEHI",
                data: "{input:'" + EMP + "'}",
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                dataType: "json",
                success: function (Result) {

                    Res = Result.d;
                    table_load8(Res);
                },

                error: function () {

                    alert('Error!');

                }
             });
            
        }

        function table_load2(data) {
            debugger;
            var valData, valData1, cnt;

            valData = data.split('Θ');

            var data = $("[id*=txt_session]").val();





            if (data != 0) {


                $('#Div7').show();
                $('#dvData').show();
                $('#submitbtn').show();
                $('#tblReportData').empty();
                $('#tblReportData').append('<thead class="bg-primary text-white" style="text-align:center;">' +


                    '<th style="text-align:center;">EMPLOYEE CODE</th> ' +

                    '<th style="text-align:center;">EMPLOYEE NAME</th> ' +

                    '<th style="text-align:center;">EMPLOYEE POST</th> ' +

                    '<th style="text-align:center;">EMPLOYEE DESIGNATION</th> ' +

                    '<th style="text-align:center;">STATUS</th> ' +

                    '</thead>/<tbody class="border border-dark">');

                debugger;


                for (i = 0; i < valData.length - 1; i++) {


                    valData1 = valData[i].split('^');


                    var AssetName = "'" + valData1[3] + "'";

                    $('#tblReportData').append('<tr>' +


                        '<td>' + valData1[0] + '</td>' +

                        '<td>' + valData1[1] + '</td>' +

                        '<td>' + valData1[2] + '</td>' +

                        '<td>' + valData1[3] + '</td>' +

                        '</tr>');

        }

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Web;
using System.Web.Services;
using System.Web.UI;
using System.Web.UI.WebControls;

using System.Data;
using System.Configuration;
using System.Data.SqlClient;
using System.IO;
using System.Collections;
using iTextSharp.text;
using iTextSharp.text.pdf;
using System.Text;
using System.Drawing;

using System.Web.Configuration;
using System.Net;
using iTextSharp.text.html.simpleparser;

namespace Ma_AppSuite.ASSETS_AND_LIABILITY
{
    public partial class Asset_And_Liability_Individual_Report : System.Web.UI.Page
    {
        static string usr = "";
        static string ip = "";
        static string hname = "";
        protected void Page_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Session["username"] as string))
            {
                Response.Redirect("~/Login.aspx");
            }
            else
            {
                usr = Session["username"].ToString();
                ip = Session["ipaddress"].ToString();
                hname = Session["hostname"].ToString();
            }
            this.txt_session.Text = usr.ToString();

        }
        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string emp_load_mt(string input)
        {
            string tblString = "";
            string s = "";
            string final = input;

            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();
            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "17", final);


            s = ds.Rows[0][0].ToString();
            if (ds.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                foreach (DataRow dr in ds.Rows)
                {
                    tblString = tblString
                        + dr[0] + "^"
                        + dr[1] + "^"
                        + dr[2] + "^"
                        + dr[3] + "^"
                         + dr[4] + "^"
                        + "Θ";
                }
            }

            return tblString.ToString();


        }
        [WebMethod(EnableSession = true)]
        public static string get_emp(string input)
        {
            string tblString = "";
            string s = "";
            string final = input;

            HRM_Service.ServiceClient obj;
            obj = new HRM_Service.ServiceClient();
            DataTable ds = new DataTable();
            ds = obj.proc_hrm_common_select("423", "15", final);
            if (ds == null || ds.Rows.Count == 0)
            {
                // If ds is null or empty, create a null row representation with column names
                tblString = "null^null^null^null^Θ"; // Adjust column names as needed
            }
            else

        private string GetHeader(DataTable dTable)
        {
            System.Text.StringBuilder dString = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            dString.Append("<thead><tr>");
            foreach (DataColumn dColumn in dTable.Columns)
            {
                dString.AppendFormat("<th>{0}</th>", dColumn.ColumnName);
            }
            dString.Append("</tr></thead>");
            return dString.ToString();
        }
        private string GetBody(DataTable dTable)
        {
            System.Text.StringBuilder dString = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            dString.Append("<tbody>");
            foreach (DataRow dRow in dTable.Rows)
            {
                dString.Append("<tr class='odd_gradeX'>");
                for (int dCount = 0; dCount < dTable.Columns.Count; dCount++)
                {
                    dString.AppendFormat("<td style=text-align:left>{0}</td>", dRow[dCount]);
                }
                dString.Append("</tr>");
            }
            dString.Append("</tbody>");
            return dString.ToString();
        }
        private string GetFooter(DataTable dTable)
        {
            System.Text.StringBuilder dString = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            dString.Append("<tfoot><tr>");
            foreach (DataColumn dColumn in dTable.Columns)
            {
                dString.AppendFormat("<th>{0}</th>", dColumn.ColumnName);
            }
            dString.Append("</tr></tfoot>");
            return dString.ToString();
        }

                dgGrid7.RenderControl(hw);












                Response.ContentType = "application/vnd.ms-excel";
                Response.AppendHeader("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=" + fname.ToString() + date + ".xls");
                this.EnableViewState = false;
                Response.Write(tw.ToString());
                Response.End();

            }


        }
        public string DataTableToHTMLTable(DataTable inTable)
        {
            System.Text.StringBuilder dString = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            dString.Append("<table id='example' class='display' cellpadding='0' cellspacing='0' border='0'>");
            dString.Append(GetHeader(inTable));
            dString.Append(GetBody(inTable));
            dString.Append("</table>");

            return "";

        }

         AND t.sequence = f.seq_id;

  elsif p_flag = 23 then
    open p_as_outresult for

        from TBL_HRM_ASSET_AND_LIABILITY_DETAILS t,
             TBL_HRM_ASSET_VEHICLES              f
       where t.emp_code = v_spliter_a(1)
         AND t.sequence = f.seq_id;

  elsif p_flag = 24 then

      into v_ecnt

using AppSuiteMVC.Repository;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using NPOI.SS.UserModel;
using System;

namespace AppSuiteMVC.Controllers
{
    public class AssetandLiabilityController : Controller
    {
        private readonly ILogger<Shift_changeController> _logger;
        private readonly DecryptionRepo _repo;
        private readonly GetDataRepo _Grepo;
        private readonly PostDataRepo _Prepo;
        private readonly string baseurl;
        private readonly string rootfolder;
        private readonly int OTPacc_id;

        private readonly IConfiguration _configuration;

        public object? Session { get; private set; }
        public string MainHeadID = "1";
        public string ResponseData = "";

        public AssetandLiabilityController(DecryptionRepo repo, GetDataRepo grepo, PostDataRepo Prepo, IConfiguration configuration)
        {
            _repo = repo;
            _Grepo = grepo;
            _Prepo = Prepo;
            _configuration = configuration;
            baseurl = _configuration.GetValue<string>("BasValues:BaseUrl") ?? "";
            rootfolder = _configuration.GetValue<string>("BasValues:root") ?? "";   
            OTPacc_id = _configuration.GetValue("BasValues:otp_acc_id", 0);

        }
        public IActionResult Index()
        {
            return View();
        }

        public string getAPIData(string datas)
        {
            string ApiPath = "hrmsmoduleapi/api/HRMSModuleAPI/GetDataHRMS/";
            ApiPath = baseurl + ApiPath;
            var token = HttpContext.Session.GetString("TokenValAPI");


            string[] DataArray = datas.Split('^');

            ViewData["baseurl"] = baseurl;
            ViewData["root"] = rootfolder;
            ViewData["HeadName"] = datas;
            string flag = DataArray[0];
            string indata = DataArray[1];
            try
            {
                var resData = _Grepo.GetInternalPageData(indata, flag, ApiPath, token);
                resData = resData.Replace(@"{""RESP"":", @"");

                return resData;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                if (ex.Message.Contains("Token has expired"))
                {
                    return null;
                }
                return null;
            }
        }


        public IActionResult master_page(string datas)
        {
            ViewData["baseurl"] = baseurl;
            ViewData["root"] = rootfolder;
            ViewData["HeadName"] = datas;

            var userId = HttpContext.Session.GetString("UserId");
            var empName = HttpContext.Session.GetString("EmpName");
            var brID = HttpContext.Session.GetString("BrID");

            ViewBag.session = HttpContext.Session.GetString("SessionVal");
            ViewBag.token = HttpContext.Session.GetString("TokenVal");

            ViewData["EmpCode"] = userId;
            ViewData["EmpName"] = empName;
            ViewData["BrID"] = brID;

            MenuListModel model = (MenuListModel)_Grepo.GetMainMenuData(userId, baseurl, MainHeadID);

            return View(model);
        }


    }

    <script>

        function goToDashboard() {

            var root = '@ViewData["root"]';

            if (document.location.hostname == 'localhost') {
                window.location.href = "/Home/Dashboard";
            }
            else {
                window.location.href = "/" + root + "/Home/Dashboard";
            }
        }
        function GetLink() {
            var root = '';
            var url = '';

            if (document.location.hostname == 'localhost') {
                url = "/Transfer/getAPIData";
            }
            else {
                root = '@ViewData["root"]';
                url = "/" + root + "/Transfer/getAPIData";
            }
            return url;
        }
        function EmpDetails() {
            _link = GetLink();
            var EmpCode= document.getElementById('code').value;
            var brid = '@ViewData["BrID"]';

            if (!EmpCode) {
                document.getElementById('name').value = '';
                return;
            }

            indata = "TRANSFER_DETAILS^TR_EMP_INFO" + "!" + 1 + '~' + EmpCode;
            var response = '';
            $.ajax({
                type: "GET",
                url: _link,
                data: { datas: indata },
                async: false,
                success: function (response) {
                    const data2 = JSON.parse(response);
                    var res1 = data2;
                    LoadEmployeeDetails(res1);
                },
                error: function (response) {
                alert("failed");
                }
            });
        }

    </script>
